Also use when the user says "debug this", "why is this failing", "fix this bug", "trace this error", or pastes stack traces, error messages, or issue references. ([Zeptiny/stupidex](https://github.com/Zeptiny/stupidex/tree/HEAD/src/stupidex/skills/defaults/debug) · ★4)
- **debug**: Find the root cause of a bug by reproducing it, testing hypotheses one at a time, fixing the cause and adding a regression test. Use when the user reports a bug, an error, a crash or unexpected behavior. ([26zl/universal-agent-skills](https://github.com/26zl/universal-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/debug) · ★3)
- **debug**: Debug and fix failing tests or errors ([abnegate/claudes](https://github.com/abnegate/claudes/tree/HEAD/skills/debug) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **debug**: Reproduce, isolate, and hypothesis-test a bug whose root cause is not yet known, until the cause is named and the failing test is obvious. Use when the symptom does not point at the fix — intermittent or flaky failures, regressions with no obvious culprit, environment-specific breakage, "not sure why". Triggered by "flaky", "intermittent", "regression", "I don't know why it's failing", "works locally but not in &lt;env&gt;", "production issue". Not for bugs whose cause is clear from the message or stack trace → `tdd` directly; not for missing or unclear requirements → `feature-doc`. ([ai-agent-lead/skills](https://github.com/ai-agent-lead/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/debug) · ★3)
- **debug**: Debugging discipline. Load when the task is fixing a bug, triaging a symptom, or chasing a failing test. Covers reproducing before reasoning, separating symptom from cause, bisecting systematically, not masking what you don't understand, suspecting assumptions, fixing the class not the instance, and leaving a regression test. ([angad-kandhari/deliberate](https://github.com/angad-kandhari/deliberate/tree/HEAD/skills/debug) · ★3)
- **debug**: Diagnose a reproducible software failure by tightening the red feedback loop, testing one hypothesis at a time, and proving root cause before recommending a fix. ([apipoj/spk](https://github.com/apipoj/spk/tree/HEAD/plugins/spk/skills/debug) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **debug**: Generate IDE debug configurations ([barnburner121/claude-plugin-marketplace](https://github.com/barnburner121/claude-plugin-marketplace/tree/HEAD/generated-plugins/debug-config/skills/debug) · ★3)
- **debug**: Systematically analyze and fix a bug. Uses hypothesis-driven debugging, records the process in spec/DEBUG.md, and updates spec/STATE.md on resolution. ([ByeongminLee/nextjs-claude-code](https://github.com/ByeongminLee/nextjs-claude-code/tree/HEAD/template/.claude/skills/debug) · ★3)
- **debug**: Debug anything — trace app runtime errors or diagnose CKS skill/agent/command issues. Language-agnostic diagnostic approach using code tracing and strategic logging.
